  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Eevee</strong></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Ability: Resonant Evolution<br>Once during your turn, when you play a Pokémon from your hand to evolve 1 of your other Eevee, you may search your deck for a card that evolves from this Pokémon and put it onto this Pokémon to evolve it. </div>

  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Oranguru V</strong></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Ability: Back Order<br>Once during your turn, if this Pokémon is in the Active Spot, you may search your deck for 2 Pokémon Tool cards, reveal them, and put them into your hand.   <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Regigigas</strong></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Ability: Ancient Wisdom<br>Once during your turn, if you have Regirock, Regice, Registeel, Regieleki, and Regidrago in play, you may attach up to 3 Energy cards from your discard pile to 1 of your Pokémon.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">[C][C][C][C][C] Gigaton Break: 150+ damage.   <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Grant</strong></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">During this turn, your [F] Pokémon’s attacks do 30 more damage to your opponent’s Active Pokémon <em>(before applying Weakness and Resistance)</em>.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">During your turn, if this Grant is in your discard pile, you may discard 2 cards, except any Grant, from your hand. </div>

  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Dark Patch</strong></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Attach a basic [D] Energy card from your discard pile to 1 of your Benched [D] Pokémon.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><em>Reprint of Dark Patch DEX 93</em></p>

  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Jubilife Village</strong></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Once during each player's turn, that player may shuffle their hand into their deck and draw 5 cards. If they do, their turn ends.</p>

  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Temple of Sinnoh</strong></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">All Special Energy attached to Pokémon <em>(both yours and your opponent’s) </em>provide [C] Energy and have no other effect.</p>
</div>
